Southampton Running Festival August 2027

Join us for the RunThrough at Southampton Running Festival August 2027 on August 8, 2027 51 WEEKS 0 DAYS 4 HRS 51 MINS 56 SECS Date Sunday, 8th August, 2027 Time 08/08/2027 09:30 - 08/08/2027 12:00 Location The Avenue, Southampton, UK Race Distance Select Distance 5K 10K Half Marathon Junior Race Enter Now

Race Entry Summary

Here are the races available for

Southampton Running Festival August 2027
  • RunThrough UK August 2027 £28 Enter Now 10K £30 Enter Now Half Marathon £36 Enter Now Junior Race £10 Enter Now

Our Partners

Join us for the Southampton Running Festival on Sunday 8th August 2027!

In Support of One Community Eastleigh

Enjoy a summer race day in the heart of the city, starting and finishing on the picturesque Southampton Common. The route takes runners through woodland, grassland, ponds, wetlands, lakes and open parkland, offering scenic surroundings and excellent PB potential. As a designated Site of Special Scientific Interest, Southampton Common creates a distinctive setting for runners, walkers and wildlife enthusiasts. Race Distance & Start Times 09:30 am - Half Marathon 10:00 am - 10k 10:05 am - 5k 12:00 pm - Junior Race Each kilometre is accurately marked with distance flags and our friendly marshals will guide and cheer you all the way to the finish. We cannot wait to welcome you to the Southampton Running Festival! With RunThrough you can expect a friendly team of marshals to help guide you round the course and cheer you on to the finish, a lively and well-serviced event village with plenty of food and drink options including free goodies from our partners at the finish line after your hard effort. We have been extensively researching what runners consider important, and sustainability is fast becoming the top issue. Click here to see what RunThrough is doing for sustainability. You can now grab your Southampton Running Festival event t-shirt for more than 30% off when you buy it as part of your entry on checkout! With the event logo printed on the chest, it’s the perfect keepsake to celebrate your race and wear your achievement with pride! We can do this with our partner RT Kit as part of a strategic decision to raise awareness about the quality of the product whilst reducing the overall cost of events for those who would prefer not to have a t-shirt included. Come and enjoy the day with us. Once you enter you will receive a confirmation email. More information will be sent closer to the event. We can’t wait to see you on the start line!

Southampton Running Festival May 2027

Join us for the RunThrough at Southampton Running Festival May 2027 on May 30, 2027 41 WEEKS 0 DAYS 4 HRS 51 MINS 15 SECS Date Sunday, 30th May, 2027 Time 30/05/2027 09:30 - 30/05/2027 12:00 Location The Avenue, Southampton, UK Race Distance Select Distance 5K 10K Half Marathon Junior Race Enter Now

Race Entry Summary

Here are the races available for

Southampton Running Festival May 2027
  • RunThrough UK May 2027 £28 Enter Now 10K £30 Enter Now Half Marathon £36 Enter Now Junior Race £10 Enter Now

Our Partners

Join us for the Southampton Running Festival on Sunday 30th May 2027!

In Support of One Community Eastleigh

Start and finish in the picturesque surroundings of Southampton Common, following a scenic route through woodland, grassland, ponds, wetlands, lakes and open parkland in the heart of the city. The late spring setting, fast course and supportive race-day atmosphere make this a brilliant opportunity to chase a personal best or enjoy a memorable run. Southampton Common is a designated Site of Special Scientific Interest and provides a unique backdrop for running, walking and wildlife watching. Race Distance & Start Times 09:30 am - Half Marathon 10:00 am - 10k 10:05 am - 5k 12:00 pm - Junior Race Each kilometre is accurately marked with distance flags and our friendly marshals will guide and cheer you all the way to the finish. We cannot wait to welcome you to the Southampton Running Festival! With RunThrough you can expect a friendly team of marshals to help guide you round the course and cheer you on to the finish, a lively and well-serviced event village with plenty of food and drink options including free goodies from our partners at the finish line after your hard effort. We have been extensively researching what runners consider important, and sustainability is fast becoming the top issue. Click here to see what RunThrough is doing for sustainability. You can now grab your Southampton Running Festival event t-shirt for more than 30% off when you buy it as part of your entry on checkout! With the event logo printed on the chest, it’s the perfect keepsake to celebrate your race and wear your achievement with pride! We can do this with our partner RT Kit as part of a strategic decision to raise awareness about the quality of the product whilst reducing the overall cost of events for those who would prefer not to have a t-shirt included. Come and enjoy the day with us. Once you enter you will receive a confirmation email. More information will be sent closer to the event. We can’t wait to see you on the start line!

Our Partners

Join us for the Leeds Running Festival on Sunday 30th May 2027!

Make the most of late spring racing with the Leeds Running Festival at Roundhay Park. With 5K, 10K and Half Marathon distances available, this event is a great opportunity to take on a scenic route through one of the biggest city parks in Europe. Expect a welcoming race-day atmosphere, beautiful park surroundings and a memorable finish. Whether you’re building towards summer goals or looking for a fun race day with friends, Leeds Running Festival is the perfect event to add to your calendar. Secure your place today and join us at Roundhay Park this May! Race Distance & Start Times Half Marathon - 09:00 AM 10K - 10:00 AM 5K - 10:00 AM Junior - 11:30 AM Included in the entry Unique themed medal Free race photos Chip-timed results Post-race goodies Each kilometre is accurately marked with distance flags, and our friendly marshals will guide and cheer you all the way to the finish. We cannot wait to welcome you to Leeds Running Festival. With RunThrough, you can expect a friendly team of marshals to help guide you around the course and cheer you on to the finish, a lively and well-serviced event village with plenty of food and drink options, including free goodies from our partners at the finish line after your hard effort. We have been extensively researching what runners consider important, and sustainability is fast becoming the top issue. Click here to see what RunThrough is doing for sustainability. You can now grab your Leeds Running Festival event t-shirt for more than 30% off when you buy it as part of your entry on checkout! With the event logo printed on the chest, it’s the perfect keepsake to celebrate your race and wear your achievement with pride! We can do this with our partner RT Kit as part of a strategic decision to raise awareness about the quality of the product, whilst reducing the overall cost of events for those who would prefer not to have a t-shirt included. Come and enjoy the day with us. Once you enter, you will receive a confirmation email. More information will be sent closer to the event. We can’t wait to see you on the start line.

Join us for Brighton TEN on Sunday 27th June 2027!

Take on a standout summer race day on the South Coast at Brighton TEN. Choose from the 10K or 10 Mile and enjoy a brilliant running experience by the sea, with the Event Village based at its new location on Hove Lawns. Whether you’re chasing a PB, stepping up to a longer distance or enjoying a summer run with friends, Brighton TEN offers a memorable race-day atmosphere in one of the UK’s most iconic coastal locations. With two exciting distance options and a lively seafront setting, this is the perfect event to add to your summer running calendar. Secure your place today and join us in Brighton this June! Race Distance & Start Times 10 Mile - 09:00 10k - 09:30 Included in the entry Unique themed medal Free race photos Chip-timed results Post-race goodies Each kilometre is accurately marked with distance flags, and our friendly marshals will guide and cheer you all the way to the finish. We cannot wait to welcome you to BRIGHTON TEN. With RunThrough, you can expect a friendly team of marshals to help guide you around the course and cheer you on to the finish, a lively and well-serviced event village with plenty of food and drink options, including free goodies from our partners at the finish line after your hard effort. We have been extensively researching what runners consider important, and sustainability is fast becoming the top issue. Click here to see what RunThrough is doing for sustainability. You can now grab your BRIGHTON TEN event t-shirt for more than 30% off when you buy it as part of your entry on checkout! With the event logo printed on the chest, it’s the perfect keepsake to celebrate your race and wear your achievement with pride!We can do this with our partner RT Kit as part of a strategic decision to raise awareness about the quality of the product, whilst reducing the overall cost of events for those who would prefer not to have a t-shirt included. Come and enjoy the day with us. Once you enter you will receive a confirmation email. More information will be sent closer to the event. We can’t wait to see you on the start line!

Take On the Marlow Triathlon at Higginson Park on Sunday 11th July 2027

In support of Macmillan Cancer Support

We’re returning to Higginson Park for the Marlow Triathlon on Sunday 11th July 2027, following the success of our event earlier this summer. Choose from Middle, Olympic or Sprint distances as you swim in the River Thames, ride through rolling hills, and run past beautiful riverside views. This summer edition promises the same electric atmosphere and top-tier racing experience. Our Partners

Take On the Marlow Triathlon at Higginson Park on Sunday 30th May 2027

In support of Macmillan Cancer Support

On Sunday 30th May 2027 the Marlow Triathlon returns for another year. This event offers Middle, Olympic, and Sprint distances ideal for both seasoned athletes and newcomers. Swim in the River Thames, cycle through the scenic Chiltern countryside, and finish strong with a riverside run across the park. With stunning views and a well-organised setup, it’s a must-do event to kick off your summer season. Whether you're taking on your first triathlon or chasing a personal best, this event offers Sprint, Olympic, and Middle distances. Each race begins with an open-water swim in the River Thames, followed by scenic bike routes through the rolling Chiltern countryside, and finishes with a run on grassy terrain in the heart of Marlow. This is your chance to race in one of the most beautiful triathlon locations in the UK. With a riverside start, ride through teh rolling hills, and a buzzing event village, the Marlow Triathlon delivers a memorable challenge for every level of athlete. Race Details & Start Times Middle Distance Triathlon - 6:30am Olympic Triathlon - 8:00am Sprint Triathlon - 9:30am Included in the entry Unique Themed Medal Free Race Photos Chip-timed results Post Race Goodies Each kilometre is accurately marked with distance flags and our friendly marshals will guide and cheer you all the way to the finish. We cannot wait to welcome you to the Marlow Triathlon. With RunThrough you can expect a friendly team of marshals to help guide you round the course and cheer you on to the finish, a lively and well-serviced event village with plenty of food and drink options including free goodies from our partners at the finish line after your hard effort. We have been extensively researching what runners consider important, and sustainability is fast becoming the top issue. Click here to see what RunThrough is doing for sustainability. You can now grab your Marlow Triathlon event t-shirt for more than 30% off when you buy it as part of your entry on checkout! With the event logo printed on the chest, it’s the perfect keepsake to celebrate your race and wear your achievement with pride!We can do this with our partner RT Kit as part of a strategic decision to raise awareness about the quality of the product whilst reducing the overall cost of events for those who would prefer not to have a t-shirt included. Come and enjoy the day with us. Once you enter you will receive a confirmation email. More information will be sent closer to the event. We can’t wait to see you on the start line! Join us for the Women’s Run Series London March 5K & 10K on Sunday 14th March 2027!

Celebrate International Women’s Day with an inspiring spring race at the Women’s Run Series London March 5K & 10K. Taking place around the iconic Queen Elizabeth Olympic Park, home of the London 2012 Olympic Games, this event is the perfect way to kickstart the 2027 Women’s Run Series. Choose from the 5K or 10K and enjoy a supportive, inclusive race-day atmosphere alongside like-minded women. The course is beginner, buggy and wheelchair accessible, making it a brilliant choice whether you’re taking on your first race, returning to running or lining up for your hundredth. Expect a welcoming event experience, plenty of encouragement and a memorable finish as we come together to celebrate women in running. Secure your place today and join us in London this March! Our Partners

Join us for Run Solihull Half Marathon, 5k & Junior Race on Sunday 8th August 2027 With a vibrant event village in Malvern Park in the heart of Solihull, the event features fully road-closed routes and a fantastic race-day atmosphere. The Half Marathon follows an undulating course through Solihull town centre before heading out to the picturesque villages of Knowle, Barston and Eastcote, offering a rewarding challenge with plenty of PB potential. The 5K provides a shorter, accessible option, while the Junior Race is the perfect opportunity for young runners to experience the excitement of race day. Super Early Bird entry – Ends 27th October 2026 Early Bird entry – Ends 7th February 2027 Standard Entry – Ends 11th July 2027 Race Distance & Start Times 09:00 am - Half Marathon Elevation - 192m | Terrain - Road, Undulating 09:35 am - 5k Elevation - 56m | Terrain - Road, Undulating 11:45 am - Junior Race Elevation - 15m | Terrain - Road, Flat Race End 12:30 pm Included in the entry Unique Themed Medal Free Race Photos Chip-timed results Post Race Goodies Each kilometre is accurately marked with distance flags and our friendly marshals will guide and cheer you all the way to the finish. We cannot wait to welcome you to the Run Solihull Half Marathon, 5k & Junior Race. With RunThrough you can expect a friendly team of marshals to help guide you round the course and cheer you on to the finish, a lively and well-serviced event village with plenty of food and drink options including free goodies from our partners at the finish line after your hard effort. We have been extensively researching what runners consider important, and sustainability is fast becoming the top issue. Click here to see what RunThrough is doing for sustainability. Our Partners

Join us for the Southport Seaside 10K on Sunday 1st August 2027!

Take on a fast, flat summer race with excellent PB potential and a welcoming course for beginners. The route follows a partially closed-road circuit, heading out and back along Marine Drive with views of the seafront and Southport Pier before returning along the Promenade to the finish in Princes Park. With coastal scenery and a speedy course, the Southport Seaside 10K is the perfect addition to your summer race calendar! Race Distance & Start Times 09:00 am - 10k Each kilometre is accurately marked with distance flags and our friendly marshals will guide and cheer you all the way to the finish. With RunThrough you can expect a friendly team of marshals to help guide you round the course and cheer you on to the finish, a lively and well-serviced event village with plenty of food and drink options including free goodies from our partners at the finish line after your hard effort. We have been extensively researching what runners consider important, and sustainability is fast becoming the top issue. Click here to see what RunThrough is doing for sustainability. Our Partners

Join us for Run Reigate Half Marathon, 10K, 5K & Kids Race on Sunday 19th September 2027!

In Support of The Brain Tumour Charity

The award-winning Run Reigate returns to Reigate Priory Park for another unforgettable day of running, community spirit and family fun. Choose from the Half Marathon, 10K, 5K or Kids Race and experience a much-loved annual event known for its fantastic scenery, unbeatable atmosphere and memorable race-day buzz. Whether you are taking on your first event, chasing a new goal or returning to enjoy the local support, Run Reigate offers something for every runner. The all-day Event Village brings even more to the day, with a wide range of food, drinks, entertainment and activities for runners, supporters and families to enjoy. The ever-popular Kids Race sells out every year, so make sure the little ones are signed up early. Secure your place today and join us at Reigate Priory Park in September 2027. Race Distance & Start Times 09:00 am - 10k 09:15 am - Half Marathon 09:20 am - 5k 12:00 pm - Kids Race Year 1 up to Year 11 13:15 pm - Inclusive Wave Each kilometre is accurately marked with distance flags and our friendly marshals will guide and cheer you all the way to the finish. We cannot wait to welcome you to the Run Reigate 5k, 10k, Half Marathon & Kids Race! With RunThrough you can expect a friendly team of marshals to help guide you round the course and cheer you on to the finish, a lively and well-serviced event village with plenty of food and drink options including free goodies from our partners at the finish line after your hard effort. We have been extensively researching what runners consider important, and sustainability is fast becoming the top issue. Click here to see what RunThrough is doing for sustainability. You can now grab your Run Reigate 5k, 10k, Half Marathon & Kids Race event t-shirt for more than 30% off when you buy it as part of your entry on checkout! With the event logo printed on the chest, it’s the perfect keepsake to celebrate your race and wear your achievement with pride! We can do this with our partner RT Kit as part of a strategic decision to raise awareness about the quality of the product whilst reducing the overall cost of events for those who would prefer not to have a t-shirt included. Come and enjoy the day with us. Once you enter you will receive a confirmation email. More information will be sent closer to the event. We can’t wait to see you on the start line! This is a partner event. RunThrough memberships can be used for entry; however, once the event sells out, no additional places will be available, including for members. Please note there will be no RunThrough member desk, and member bibs cannot be used at this event.

Our Partners

Join Us for RunThrough Trails South Downs on Sunday 1st August 2027!

Experience one of the UK's most iconic trail running destinations at RunThrough Trails South Downs on Sunday 1st August 2027. Starting from Queen Elizabeth Country Park, you'll take on a spectacular route through the rolling hills, ancient woodland and open chalk downland of the South Downs National Park. Choose your challenge from 12K, 25K or 50K, with each route showcasing some of the region's finest trails, including sections of the famous South Downs Way. Whether you're stepping into trail running for the first time or preparing for your next ultra-distance adventure, there's a distance to suit every level of runner. Expect grassy tracks, woodland paths and rewarding climbs that open up to sweeping views across Hampshire and Sussex. Enjoy fast-flowing descents, beautiful countryside and a true escape into nature before returning to the welcoming event village. With clear waymarking, well-stocked aid stations, supportive marshals and the friendly atmosphere you've come to expect from RunThrough Trails, you'll have everything you need for an unforgettable day on the trails. Whether you're chasing a new challenge, building confidence on technical terrain or simply looking to explore one of England's most beautiful national parks, RunThrough Trails South Downs promises an incredible trail running experience from start to finish. Race Distance & Start Times 50k - 08:00 25k - 10:00 12k - 11:00 Included in the entry Unique themed medal Free race photos Chip-timed results Post-race goodies All routes will be marked using trail flags and tags, along with A5 arrows and signage. Our friendly marshals will also be on hand to guide you at major junctions and cheer you all the way to the finish. However, please note with all trail races, routes can be disrupted by many things: the weather, disgruntled walkers and wildlife, to name a few! To keep you safe, we provide the GPX for your race route so you can check you’re on track throughout the race and course correct/self-navigate if necessary. With RunThrough, you can expect a friendly team of marshals to help guide you around the course and cheer you on to the finish, a lively and well-serviced event village with plenty of food and drink options, including free goodies from our partners at the finish line after your hard effort. We have been extensively researching what runners consider important, and sustainability is fast becoming the top issue. Click here to see what RunThrough is doing for sustainability. You can now grab your RunThrough Trails South Downs event t-shirt for more than 30% off when you buy it as part of your entry on checkout! With the event logo printed on the chest, it’s the perfect keepsake to celebrate your race and wear your achievement with pride!We can do this with our partner RT Kit as part of a strategic decision to raise awareness about the quality of the product, whilst reducing the overall cost of events for those who would prefer not to have a t-shirt included. Come and enjoy the day with us. Once you enter, you will receive a confirmation email. More information will be sent closer to the event. We can’t wait to see you on the start line.

Choose from the 10K, 10 Mile or Junior Race and enjoy a popular road-closed event through the beautiful Cheshire countryside. Whether you’re chasing a new PB, stepping up your distance or bringing the family along for the Junior Race, this event offers a friendly atmosphere and a memorable morning of running. With scenic routes, a supportive race-day buzz and a unique medal waiting at the finish, West Cheshire TEN is one to add to your summer running calendar. Our Partners

Join us for the RunThrough USA Long Beach 5K, 10K & Half Marathon on Saturday 3rd April 2027!

Starting from Shoreline Aquatic Park in Long Beach, California, this scenic race offers 5K, 10K and Half Marathon distances along one of LA’s most spectacular coastal locations. With fast routes, beautiful waterfront views and an exciting race-day atmosphere, it is the perfect opportunity to chase a personal best or enjoy a memorable run by the coast. Race Start Times Half Marathon - 8:00am10k - 8:30am5k - 8:45am Terrain Pave Bike Path Race Maps 5k / 10k / Half Marathon Included in the entry Unique Themed Medal Chip Timed Results Post-race goodies Free Race Photos Each mile is accurately marked with distance flags and our friendly marshals will guide and cheer you all the way to the finish. We cannot wait to welcome you to Long Beach! With RunThrough you can expect a friendly team of marshals to help guide you round the course and cheer you on to the finish, a lively and well-serviced event village with plenty of food and drink options including free goodies from our partners at the finish line after your hard effort. We have been extensively researching what runners consider important, and sustainability is fast becoming the top issue. Click here to see what RunThrough is doing for sustainability. You can now grab your Long Beach event t-shirt for more than 30% off when you buy it as part of your entry on checkout! With the event logo printed on the chest, it’s the perfect keepsake to celebrate your race and wear your achievement with pride! We can do this with our partner RT Kit as part of a strategic decision to raise awareness about the quality of the product whilst reducing the overall cost of events for those who would prefer not to have a t-shirt included. Come and enjoy the day with us. Once you enter you will receive a confirmation email. More information will be sent closer to the event. We can’t wait to see you on the start line!

Join Us for the Carlisle Half Marathon, 10K & 5K on Sunday 18th July 2027

Experience an exciting day of racing at the Carlisle Half Marathon, 10K & 5K on Sunday 18th July 2027. Set entirely within the grounds of Carlisle Racecourse, this unique event offers a lively atmosphere, fantastic spectator support and a professionally organised race day for runners of all abilities. Choose from the Half Marathon, 10K or 5K, with each distance following a fully contained multi-lap course that keeps you close to the event village throughout your run. The format creates a brilliant atmosphere, giving supporters plenty of opportunities to cheer you on as you work towards the finish line. Whether you're chasing a new personal best, stepping up to a new distance or taking part in your first organised race, you'll enjoy a welcoming event with accurately measured routes, chip timing, clear course marking and supportive marshals from start to finish. With a vibrant race-day atmosphere and one of Cumbria's most recognisable sporting venues as the backdrop, the Carlisle Half Marathon, 10K & 5K is the perfect way to enjoy a summer day of running. Secure your place today and join us at Carlisle Racecourse this July. Sign up today and be part of this exciting summer race! Each kilometre is accurately marked with distance flags and our friendly marshals will guide and cheer you all the way to the finish. Edinburgh Running Festival August 2027

Join us for the RunThrough at Edinburgh Running Festival August 2027 on August 1, 2027 50 WEEKS 0 DAYS 4 HRS 45 MINS 14 SECS Sunday, 1st August, 2027 01/08/2027 10:55 - 01/08/2027 13:00 Queen's Dr, Edinburgh EH8 8HG, UK Race Distance Select Distance 5K 10K Half Marathon

Race Entry Summary

Here are the races available for RunThrough UK August 2027 £30 10K £34 Half Marathon £36

Our Partners

Join Us for the Edinburgh Running Festival on Sunday 1st August 2027!

Experience one of Scotland's most spectacular race days at the Edinburgh Running Festival on Sunday 1st August 2027. Choose from a Half Marathon, 10K or 5K and take on a stunning fully road-closed route through the iconic Holyrood Park. Run beneath the towering Arthur's Seat and the dramatic Salisbury Crags, with sweeping views across Edinburgh's historic skyline throughout your race. The undulating course offers a rewarding challenge for runners of all abilities, whether you're chasing a personal best or simply soaking up the atmosphere in one of the UK's most breathtaking running locations. Whether you're visiting Edinburgh for the first time or returning to race through one of Scotland's most iconic landscapes, the Edinburgh Running Festival offers an unforgettable running experience. Secure your place today and experience Edinburgh like never before. Race Distance & Start Times 10:00 - Half Marathon 10:45 - 10k 10:55 - 5k Laps Half Marathon (4 Laps) 10K (2 laps) 5K (1 Lap) Included in the entry Unique themed medal Free race photos Chip-timed results Post-race goodies Each kilometre or mile is accurately marked with distance flags, and our friendly marshals will guide and cheer you all the way to the finish. We cannot wait to welcome you to the Edinburgh Running Festival. With RunThrough, you can expect a friendly team of marshals to help guide you around the course and cheer you on to the finish, a lively and well-serviced event village with plenty of food and drink options, including free goodies from our partners at the finish line after your hard effort. We have been extensively researching what runners consider important, and sustainability is fast becoming the top issue. Click here to see what RunThrough is doing for sustainability. You can now grab your Edinburgh Running Festival event t-shirt for more than 30% off when you buy it as part of your entry on checkout! With the event logo printed on the chest, it’s the perfect keepsake to celebrate your race and wear your achievement with pride!We can do this with our partner RT Kit as part of a strategic decision to raise awareness about the quality of the product, whilst reducing the overall cost of events for those who would prefer not to have a t-shirt included. Come and enjoy the day with us. Once you enter, you will receive a confirmation email. More information will be sent closer to the event. We can’t wait to see you on the start line. Windsor Trail Half Marathon & 10k April 2027

Join us for the RunThrough at Windsor Trail Half Marathon & 10k April 2027 on April 4, 2027 33 WEEKS 0 DAYS 4 HRS 44 MINS 21 SECS Date Sunday, 4th April, 2027 Time 04/04/2027 09:00 - 04/04/2027 12:20 Location 3 Alma Rd, Windsor SL4 3HU, UK Race Distance Select Distance 10K Half Marathon Enter Now

Windsor Trail Half Marathon & 10k April 2027

Our Partners

Join us for the Windsor Trail Run on Sunday 4th April 2027!

In Support of Brain Research UK

Step into spring with a beautiful trail run through one of the UK’s most historic locations. Choose from the 10K or Half Marathon and enjoy a flat trail course through Alexandra Gardens, with Windsor Castle providing a stunning backdrop along the way. Whether you’re taking on your first trail event, stepping up your distance or looking for a memorable spring race, Windsor Trail Run is a brilliant choice. Expect scenic paths, a welcoming atmosphere and a finish line to remember. Secure your place today and join us in Windsor this April! Race Distance & Start Times 09:00 am - 10k 09:20 am - Half Marathon Included in the entry Free Race Photos Chip-timed results Post Race Goodies Unique Themed Medal Each kilometre is accurately marked with distance flags and our friendly marshals will guide and cheer you all the way to the finish. We cannot wait to welcome you to the Windsor Trail Half Marathon & 10k. With RunThrough you can expect a friendly team of marshals to help guide you round the course and cheer you on to the finish, a lively and well-serviced event village with plenty of food and drink options including free goodies from our partners at the finish line after your hard effort. We have been extensively researching what runners consider important, and sustainability is fast becoming the top issue. Click here to see what RunThrough is doing for sustainability. You can now grab your Windsor Trail Half Marathon & 10k event t-shirt for more than 30% off when you buy it as part of your entry on checkout! With the event logo printed on the chest, it’s the perfect keepsake to celebrate your race and wear your achievement with pride!We can do this with our partner RT Kit as part of a strategic decision to raise awareness about the quality of the product whilst reducing the overall cost of events for those who would prefer not to have a t-shirt included. Come and enjoy the day with us. Once you enter you will receive a confirmation email. More information will be sent closer to the event. We can’t wait to see you on the start line!

Our Partners

Join us for the Windsor Trail Run on Sunday 31st January 2027!

In Support of Brain Research UK

Start your 2027 running year with a scenic winter trail run through Windsor. Offering 10K and Half Marathon distances, the Windsor Trail Run takes you along the beautifully kept paths of Alexandra Gardens, with Windsor Castle towering in the distance. The flat trail course is ideal for runners of all abilities, whether you’re chasing an early-year goal, building your fitness or enjoying a crisp winter race day. Take in the historic surroundings, enjoy the supportive atmosphere and cross the finish line in one of the UK’s most scenic running locations. Secure your place today and join us in Windsor this January! Race Distance & Start Times 09:00 am - 10k 09:20 am - Half Marathon Included in the entry Free Race Photos Chip-timed results Post Race Goodies Unique Themed Medal Each kilometre is accurately marked with distance flags and our friendly marshals will guide and cheer you all the way to the finish. We cannot wait to welcome you to the Windsor Trail Half Marathon & 10k. With RunThrough you can expect a friendly team of marshals to help guide you round the course and cheer you on to the finish, a lively and well-serviced event village with plenty of food and drink options including free goodies from our partners at the finish line after your hard effort. We have been extensively researching what runners consider important, and sustainability is fast becoming the top issue. Click here to see what RunThrough is doing for sustainability. You can now grab your Windsor Trail Half Marathon & 10k event t-shirt for more than 30% off when you buy it as part of your entry on checkout! With the event logo printed on the chest, it’s the perfect keepsake to celebrate your race and wear your achievement with pride!We can do this with our partner RT Kit as part of a strategic decision to raise awareness about the quality of the product whilst reducing the overall cost of events for those who would prefer not to have a t-shirt included. Come and enjoy the day with us. Once you enter you will receive a confirmation email. More information will be sent closer to the event. We can’t wait to see you on the start line! Join us for the Windsor Trail Run on Sunday 29th August 2027!

In Support of Brain Research UK

Make the most of the summer bank holiday weekend with a scenic run through Windsor. The Windsor Trail Run offers 10K and Half Marathon distances on a flat trail course through the beautifully kept paths of Alexandra Gardens, with views towards the iconic Windsor Castle. It’s the perfect event for runners of all abilities looking to enjoy a late-summer challenge in a standout location. Take in the sights, soak up the atmosphere and enjoy a memorable race day in one of the UK’s most historic settings. Secure your place today and join us in Windsor this August! Race Distance & Start Times 09:00 am - 10k 09:20 am - Half Marathon Included in the entry Free Race Photos Chip-timed results Post Race Goodies Unique Themed Medal Each kilometre is accurately marked with distance flags and our friendly marshals will guide and cheer you all the way to the finish. We cannot wait to welcome you to the Windsor Trail Half Marathon & 10k. With RunThrough you can expect a friendly team of marshals to help guide you round the course and cheer you on to the finish, a lively and well-serviced event village with plenty of food and drink options including free goodies from our partners at the finish line after your hard effort. We have been extensively researching what runners consider important, and sustainability is fast becoming the top issue. Click here to see what RunThrough is doing for sustainability. Run Alton Towers 5k, 10K & Junior Race on 14th November 2026

Join us at the UK’s biggest theme park for a brilliant morning of racing and rides. The Run Alton Towers 5k, 10K and Junior Race take you on a unique route inside Alton Towers Resort, with a finish you will remember and theme park entry included in every place. Celebrate your run with a full day of fun on the rides after you cross the line. Set on smooth, fully marked paths within the resort, the course is designed for flow and enjoyment. Expect clear signage with KM flags and mile markers, friendly marshals at key points, and a lively event village atmosphere to keep the energy high from start to finish. Trail Running Workshop September 2026

Join us for the RunThrough at Trail Running Workshop September 2026 on September 19, 2026 4 WEEKS 6 DAYS 4 HRS 43 MINS 13 SECS Saturday, 19th September, 2026 19/09/2026 09:15 - 19/09/2026 16:15 37 Main St, Newtown Linford, Leicester LE6 0AD, UK Race Distance Select Distance Workshop

Race Entry Summary

Here are the races available for

Trail Running Workshop September 2026
  • RunThrough UK September 2026 Workshop £95

Our Partners

Join us for the Trail Running Workshop on Saturday 19th September 2026!

Level up your trail running skills with a full-day workshop designed to help you build confidence, improve technique and feel more prepared on the trails. The day will begin with a coached running session focused on uphill and downhill running techniques, giving you the chance to put your skills into practice out on the trails. You’ll then move into educational sessions covering GPX and map-reading skills, route planning with your watch and training strategies specific to trail running. Throughout the day, expert coaches will guide you through each session, helping you understand how to apply what you learn to your own running journey and build a stronger, more efficient trail running body. Ticket Selling Early Bird Offer: £95 until 19th August 2026 Standard Entry: £110 after 19th August 2026 Secure your place today and join us for a practical, confidence-building day on the trails. London Running Festival December 2026

Join us for the RunThrough at London Running Festival December 2026 on December 6, 2026 16 WEEKS 0 DAYS 5 HRS 42 MINS 58 SECS Sunday, 6th December, 2026 06/12/2026 09:00 - 06/12/2026 12:00 GXXJ+M52, London, UK Race Distance Select Distance 10K Half Marathon 5K Ballot Application 5K Guaranteed Place

Race Entry Summary

Here are the races available for

London Running Festival December 2026
  • RunThrough UK December 2026 10K £28 Half Marathon £32 5K Ballot Application £0 5K Guaranteed Place £50

Our Partners

Join us for the London Running Festival on Sunday 6th December 2026!

Celebrate the festive season with a road-closed race day at Queen Elizabeth Olympic Park for the London Running Festival. Choose from a FREE 5K, with around 500 places available, or take on the paid 10K or Half Marathon distances through one of London’s most iconic sporting venues. For the first time, the event village will be based at Hopkins Meadow in North Park, creating a brilliant festive race-day hub for runners and supporters. The routes will take runners past some of Queen Elizabeth Olympic Park’s standout landmarks, including the VeloPark, Copper Box Arena, Aquatics Centre, London Stadium, London Community Track, South Park and North Park. With fully road-closed routes, a fantastic atmosphere and a festive feel throughout, this is the perfect way to round off your 2026 running calendar. Secure your place today and join us at Queen Elizabeth Olympic Park this December! Ticket Selling Super Early Bird entry - Ends Sunday 4th October 2026 Early Bird entry - Ends Sunday 29th December 2026

Free RunThrough Foundation 5K Ballot

Entry to the free RunThrough Foundation 5K is by ballot due to expected high demand. In 2025, over 50,000 people applied for 6,000 places in the Foundation 10K, so the ballot helps keep entry genuinely accessible rather than becoming a race to click first. A proportion of places will be prioritised for first-time runners and people from lower income areas, in line with the RunThrough Foundation’s mission to break down barriers to entry. There will be three ballot waves: Ballot Wave 1 Closes: 30th August 2026Announced: 3rd September 2026 Ballot Wave 2 Closes: 25th October 2026Announced: 5th November 2026 Ballot Wave 3 Closes: 22nd November 2026Announced: 26th November 2026 Entering the ballot does not automatically enter you into the event. After each ballot wave, we will contact applicants with the outcome. If you are successful, you will need to accept your place. Any places not accepted will be passed on to another applicant. Only one application per email address will be accepted. Multiple entries under the same email address will not be processed. RunThrough Trails Morzine-Avoriaz June 2027

Race Entry Summary

RunThrough Trails Morzine-Avoriaz June 2027

Our Partners

Join us for RunThrough Trails Ultra Trail Morzine-Avoriaz on Saturday 19th June 2027!

Take on an unforgettable Alpine trail running experience in the heart of the French Alps with RunThrough Trails Ultra Trail Morzine-Avoriaz, part of the RunThrough Trails Global Series. Set within the breathtaking Portes du Soleil region, the routes showcase the very best of Morzine-Avoriaz, from established village trails and forest paths to high Alpine terrain and sweeping mountain views. Runners will pass through beech and fir forests, alongside lakes and out above the treeline, taking on a mix of fast-flowing tracks, technical climbs and rewarding descents. Choose from 25K, 50K or 100K distances, each offering a unique challenge across the eastern side of the Morzine Valley and through Avoriaz. Whether you’re stepping into Alpine trail running or taking on your next ultra challenge, Ultra Trail Morzine-Avoriaz offers an incredible setting, challenging terrain and a race-day experience to remember. Secure your place today and discover the trails of Morzine-Avoriaz!

Our Partners

Run Reigate 5k, 10k, Half Marathon & Kids Race 20th September 2026

In Support of The Brain Tumour Charity

The award-winning Run Reigate Half Marathon, 10K, 5K and Kids Race will be back on Sunday 20 September 2026 in Reigate Priory Park. Entries are now open. Sponsored by Simply Sports, this much loved sell-out annual running and community event guarantees you fantastic scenery, an unbeatable atmosphere and a memorable experience. Our huge all day Event Village has something for everyone with a wide range of top notch food, drinks, entertainment and activities – don’t miss it. Whether you’re a beginner, a seasoned runner or a veteran Run Reigate runner who loves the local scenery, fresh air and a fantastic buzz, Run Reigate is our much loved community event. The ever popular Kids Race sells out every year so make sure you get the little ones signed up early. Race Distance & Start Times 09:00 am - 10k 09:15 am - Half Marathon 09:20 am - 5k 12:00 pm - Kids Race Year 1 up to Year 11 Race Ends 13:00 pm Each kilometre is accurately marked with distance flags and our friendly marshals will guide and cheer you all the way to the finish. We cannot wait to welcome you to the Run Reigate 5k, 10k, Half Marathon & Kids Race! With RunThrough you can expect a friendly team of marshals to help guide you round the course and cheer you on to the finish, a lively and well-serviced event village with plenty of food and drink options including free goodies from our partners at the finish line after your hard effort. We have been extensively researching what runners consider important, and sustainability is fast becoming the top issue. Click here to see what RunThrough is doing for sustainability. You can now grab your Run Reigate 5k, 10k, Half Marathon & Kids Race event t-shirt for more than 30% off when you buy it as part of your entry on checkout! With the event logo printed on the chest, it’s the perfect keepsake to celebrate your race and wear your achievement with pride! We can do this with our partner RT Kit as part of a strategic decision to raise awareness about the quality of the product whilst reducing the overall cost of events for those who would prefer not to have a t-shirt included. Come and enjoy the day with us. Once you enter you will receive a confirmation email. More information will be sent closer to the event. We can’t wait to see you on the start line! This is a partner event. RunThrough memberships can be used for entry; however, once the event sells out, no additional places will be available, including for members. Please note there will be no RunThrough member desk, and member bibs cannot be used at this event. Loughborough Training Night August 2026

Join us for the RunThrough at Loughborough Training Night August 2026 on August 28, 2026 1 WEEKS 5 DAYS 4 HRS 42 MINS 0 SECS Date Friday, 28th August, 2026 Time 28/08/2026 18:15 - 28/08/2026 19:30 Location Rutland Way, Loughborough LE11 3TP, UK Race Distance Select Distance Training Night Enter Now

SIGN UP NOW

Join us at Loughborough Training Night August 2026

Training Night £0 Enter Now

Our Partners

Join us for the Loughborough Training Night on Friday 28th August 2026!

Take your training to the next level with a free coach-led session at the Paula Radcliffe Athletics Stadium, Loughborough University, delivered by our experienced RunThrough Coaches. This structured track session is designed to support efficiency over shorter distances, helping runners build confidence, improve running mechanics and understand how to apply key endurance training principles. Participants will also take part in guided running drills focused on movement skills, running efficiency and stronger overall performance. The session will include: 2 x 90 seconds with 90 seconds rest 4 x 60 seconds with 60 seconds rest 4 x 30 seconds with 30 seconds rest 4 x 15 seconds with 15 seconds rest The focus of the session is training the body to clear lactate at higher intensities, helping you feel stronger, sharper and more efficient when running at faster paces. Whether you’re looking to improve your speed, refine your form or learn from experienced coaches in a supportive environment, this free Loughborough Training Night is a great opportunity to boost your running. Secure your free place today and join us on the track in Loughborough.

Join us at Manchester Training Night August 2026

Training Night £0

Join us for the Manchester Training Night on Tuesday 25th August 2026!

Take your training further with a free coach-led session at Longford Park Stadium, Manchester, delivered by our experienced RunThrough Coaches. This structured training night is designed to help runners improve endurance, pacing and running efficiency through a pyramid fartlek session, alongside guided running drills focused on movement skills, form and confidence. Our coaches will also share practical insights into the key principles of endurance training and effective pacing. This pyramid fartlek session is designed to develop aerobic capacity, speed endurance and lactate tolerance by gradually increasing and then decreasing effort durations within one continuous session. It helps runners practise controlled pacing, manage fatigue and maintain good form when working across different intensities. Whether you’re looking to sharpen your race-day pacing, improve your efficiency or learn from experienced coaches in a supportive environment, this free Manchester Training Night is a great opportunity to boost your running. Secure your free place today and join us on the track in Manchester. Route Information x * Route Information * What's Included * How To Get There * Event Day Logistics

Our Partners

Join us for the Wolverhampton 10K on Sunday 7th February 2027!

Take on a scenic and one-of-a-kind 10K race through Wolverhampton, starting and finishing at the iconic Molineux Stadium. This closed-road route gives runners the chance to experience the city in a unique way, with a memorable stadium setting, fantastic local support and a brilliant race-day atmosphere from start to finish. Whether you’re chasing a new PB, taking on your first 10K or looking for an exciting early-season challenge, the Wolverhampton 10K is the perfect event to add to your 2027 race calendar. Secure your place today and join us on the start line in Wolverhampton! Start Time 09:00 am - 10k Included in the entry Unique Themed Medal Chip Timed Results Post-race goodies Free Official Race photos Each kilometre is accurately marked with distance flags and our friendly marshals will guide and cheer you all the way to the finish. We cannot wait to welcome you to the Wolverhampton 10k. With RunThrough you can expect a friendly team of marshals to help guide you round the course and cheer you on to the finish, a lively and well-serviced event village with plenty of food and drink options including free goodies from our partners at the finish line after your hard effort. We have been extensively researching what runners consider important, and sustainability is fast becoming the top issue. Click here to see what RunThrough is doing for sustainability. You can now grab your Wolverhampton event t-shirt for more than 30% off when you buy it as part of your entry on checkout! With the event logo printed on the chest, it’s the perfect keepsake to celebrate your race and wear your achievement with pride! We can do this with our partner RT Kit as part of a strategic decision to raise awareness about the quality of the product whilst reducing the overall cost of events for those who would prefer not to have a t-shirt included. Come and enjoy the day with us. Once you enter you will receive a confirmation email. More information will be sent closer to the event. We can’t wait to see you on the start line!

Join us for Ride Reigate on Sunday 11th July 2027!

In Support of The Brain Tumour Charity

Take on a brilliant summer cycling challenge through the beautiful Surrey and Sussex countryside at Ride Reigate 2027. With three sportive distances available, this event is perfect for cyclists looking to enjoy scenic rural roads, rolling countryside and a well-supported ride in the heart of the South East. Whether you are taking on your first sportive or returning for a bigger challenge, Ride Reigate offers a memorable day on the bike. The 100K route also includes a timed section at Kidd’s Hill, giving riders the chance to test themselves on one of the standout climbs of the course. London Half & 10k Presented by TREK February 2027

Join us for the RunThrough at London Half & 10k Presented by TREK February 2027 on February 28, 2027 28 WEEKS 0 DAYS 5 HRS 40 MINS 29 SECS Sunday, 28th February, 2027 28/02/2027 09:00 - 28/02/2027 12:30 London E20 2ST, UK Race Distance Select Distance 10K Half Marathon Junior Race

Race Entry Summary

Here are the races available for

London Half & 10k Presented by TREK February 2027
  • RunThrough UK February 2027 10K £31 Half Marathon £36 Junior Race £10

Our Partners

Join us for the London Half & 10K presented by TREK on Sunday 28th February 2027!

In Support of Papyrus

Take on an unforgettable late-winter race experience in the heart of East London at Queen Elizabeth Olympic Park, home of the London 2012 Olympic Games. Whether you are chasing a new PB, building towards your spring goals or soaking up the historic sporting atmosphere, the London Half & 10K offers a flat, scenic route filled with Olympic legacy. Runners of all ages and abilities are welcome, with Half Marathon, 10K and Junior race options available. Ticket Selling Super Early Bird entry - Ends Sunday 30th August 2026 Early Bird entry - Ends Sunday 27th October 2026 Standard entry - Ends Sunday 31st January 2027 Race Distance & Start Times 09:00 am - Half Marathon 09:38 am - 10k 12:00 pm- Junior race Race End 12:30 pm Included in the entry Unique Themed Medal Free Race Photos Chip-timed results Post Race Goodies Celebrate London’s Olympic legacy with every step. Whether it’s your first race or you’re a seasoned runner, the London Half & 10K is a memorable way to kick off your running season. Sign up today and be part of something special! Each kilometre is accurately marked with distance flags and our friendly marshals will guide and cheer you all the way to the finish. We cannot wait to welcome you to the London Half Marathon & 10k Presented by TREK. With RunThrough you can expect a friendly team of marshals to help guide you round the course and cheer you on to the finish, a lively and well-serviced event village with plenty of food and drink options including free goodies from our partners at the finish line after your hard effort. We have been extensively researching what runners consider important, and sustainability is fast becoming the top issue. Click here to see what RunThrough is doing for sustainability. You can now grab your London Half & 10k Presented by TREK event t-shirt for more than 30% off when you buy it as part of your entry on checkout! With the event logo printed on the chest, it’s the perfect keepsake to celebrate your race and wear your achievement with pride! We can do this with our partner RT Kit as part of a strategic decision to raise awareness about the quality of the product whilst reducing the overall cost of events for those who would prefer not to have a t-shirt included. Come and enjoy the day with us. Once you enter you will receive a confirmation email. More information will be sent closer to the event. We can’t wait to see you on the start line! Morpeth 10k June 2027

Join us for the RunThrough at Morpeth 10k June 2027 on June 27, 2027 45 WEEKS 0 DAYS 4 HRS 40 MINS 13 SECS Sunday, 27th June, 2027 27/06/2027 09:00 - 27/06/2027 11:30 Market Pl, Morpeth NE61 1HG, UK Race Distance Select Distance RunThrough Foundation Place (no Fundraising Required) 10K A-race (sub 40 Mins) 10K B-race Junior Race

Race Entry Summary

Here are the races available for

Morpeth 10k June 2027
  • RunThrough UK June 2027 RunThrough Foundation Place (No fundraising required) £80 10k A-Race (Sub 40 mins) £32 10k B-Race £32 Junior Race £10

Our Partners

Join us for the Morpeth 10K & Juniors on Sunday 27th June 2027!

Starting and finishing beside Morpeth’s historic clock tower, this closed-road race offers a brilliant early summer challenge in the heart of Northumberland. The predominantly flat 10K route takes in key landmarks around the town before heading onto a picturesque 2-lap loop through the surrounding countryside. The course is officially measured, making it a fantastic choice for runners chasing a new PB, while the supportive, community-led atmosphere makes it just as welcoming for those taking on the distance for the first time. To keep the course flowing comfortably, the 10K will use two start waves. Choose the wave that best matches your expected finish time so you can run alongside others at a similar pace: 10K A-Race: Sub 40 minutes 10K B-Race: Over 40 minutes The Junior Race gives younger runners the chance to be part of the Morpeth race-day experience, with plenty of support and encouragement from the crowd. Race Distance & Start Times10k A race - 9:00 am 10k B race - 9:25 am Juniors - 10:45 am Included in the entry Unique themed medal Free race photos Chip-timed results Post-race goodies Each kilometre is accurately marked with distance flags, and our friendly marshals will guide and cheer you all the way to the finish. We cannot wait to welcome you to Morpeth 10k. With RunThrough, you can expect a friendly team of marshals to help guide you around the course and cheer you on to the finish, a lively and well-serviced event village with plenty of food and drink options, including free goodies from our partners at the finish line after your hard effort. We have been extensively researching what runners consider important, and sustainability is fast becoming the top issue. Click here to see what RunThrough is doing for sustainability.
